### [75] [Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19234)
*Eddy Soria,Aida Valls,Ana Beatriz Hernández-Lara*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一种将线性重缩放直接整合进三角模糊数距离计算的度量 d_TR，证明其满足度量性质且具备有界性、尺度不变性与原点不变性，适用于异构模糊数据场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三角模糊数距离度量通常假设数据同尺度，处理异构属性时需额外归一化步骤，缺乏将归一化内嵌于距离计算的统一方案。

Method: 设计 Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_TR)，在线性重缩放框架下直接计算距离，并引入权重向量以支持维度优先级。

Result: 形式化证明 d_TR 满足非负性、同一性、对称性与三角不等式，且具备有界性、尺度不变性与原点不变性。

Conclusion: d_TR 为异构模糊数据提供稳健的距离度量，可支撑合成指标构建、基于距离的机器学习及多准则决策辅助等应用。

Abstract: Decision-making in complex systems often involves dealing with imprecise or uncertain information, frequently represented using fuzzy sets, particularly Triangular Fuzzy Numbers (TFNs). A crucial aspect of many fuzzy methods is the quantification of distance between TFNs. Many distance measures assume that all values are in the same scale, requiring a preliminary normalization stage when applied to heterogeneous attributes with different scales or units. This paper proposes the Triangular Fuzzy Rescaling Distance (d_{TR}), a metric designed to address this challenge. The d_{TR} uniquely integrates Linear Rescaling (LRE) directly into the distance calculation, ensuring normalization during the comparison of fuzzy numbers. We formally prove that d_{TR} satisfies the properties of a metric, including non-negativity, identity, symmetry, and the triangle inequality. Furthermore, we demonstrate that d_{TR} is bounded, scale-invariant, and origin-invariant. These properties, combined with a weighting vector for prioritizing dimensions, make d_{TR} suitable for applications involving heterogeneous fuzzy data, such as the construction of synthetic indicators, distance-based machine learning algorithms or multicriteria-decision aiding.

</details>


### [76] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出将AI意识问题转向AI效价问题，通过评估AI是否具备构成效价体验的状态，为可能具有意识的AI发展提供负责任的处理方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对AI意识可能性的深刻不确定性，现有处理方式陷入两难：假设无意识可能伤害有道德地位的实体，假设有意识则可能浪费资源于无感知机器，而AI意识问题的不可解性使这一困境难以摆脱。

Method: 建议将问题从不可解的AI意识转向可解的AI效价，即评估AI是否具有若其有意识则构成效价体验的状态，以此作为道德决策的基础。

Result: 证明这种效价评估足以支撑对可能具有意识的AI发展的负责任态度。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦AI效价而非意识，可为处理潜在有感知AI提供可行且负责任的实践路径。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [77] [Longitudinal Bayesian Learning of Continuous Disease Position across the Alzheimer's Disease Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19436)
*Yingying Zhang,Kun Zhao,Guodong Liu,Qi Huang,Pengfei Gu,Dongchul Kim,Erik Enriquez,Alex D. Leow,Paul M. Thompson,Heng Huang,Hongchang Gao,Liang Zhan,Haoteng Tang*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出DCP框架，从纵向DTI中估计AD连续病程位置并量化不确定性，DCS优于现有方法且具临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD是连续生物学过程，现有神经影像AI多局限于横断面离散诊断或评分预测，无法刻画连续进展。

Method: 纵向贝叶斯学习框架，将疾病严重度建模为低维概率潜变量，融合纵向观测与弱临床监督，导出DCS及其不确定性。

Result: 在ADNI上优于代表性进展方法；DCS准确反映严重度、强临床相关性、保持纵向演化并预测未来转化。

Conclusion: DCS提供超越传统诊断标签和临床评分的定量影像表征，支持AD连续进展评估。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progresses as a continuous biological process, whereas most existing neuroimaging-based artificial intelligence methods remain limited to discrete diagnosis or clinical score prediction from cross-sectional imaging. In this work, we propose Disease Continuum Positioning (DCP), a longitudinal Bayesian Learning framework that continuously estimates disease severity from longitudinal diffusion tensor imaging (DTI). Specifically, DCP models disease severity as a low-dimensional probabilistic latent variable by jointly integrating longitudinal observations with weak clinical supervision, from which the proposed Disease Continuum Score (DCS) is derived to quantify an individual's position along the Alzheimer's disease continuum together with its associated uncertainty. Extensive experiments on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) cohort demonstrate that DCP consistently outperforms representative disease progression methods. More importantly, comprehensive validation analyses show that DCS accurately characterizes disease severity, exhibits strong clinical relevance, preserves longitudinal disease evolution, and predicts future disease conversion. These results suggest that DCS provides a quantitative imaging-derived representation for continuous assessment of Alzheimer's disease progression beyond conventional diagnostic labels and clinical scores.

</details>


### [78] [ShadowPath: Lookup-Private Credential Status Verification over Authenticated State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19937)
*Patrick Herbke,Wolf Rieder,Christian René Sechting,Huaning Yang,Sid Lamichhane,Philip Raschke,Axel Küpper*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: ShadowPath 将可验证凭证的撤销检查移至持有者端，通过零知识证明在保护隐私的同时完成状态验证，并首次评估了 Verkle 树在凭证撤销中的应用，发现其证明成本高于稀疏 Merkle 树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有撤销检查可能泄露重复标识符、注册表位置或请求元数据，这些信息可作为稳定句柄关联不同演示，破坏隐私模型。

Method: 持有者针对验证者选定的注册表根，在零知识中证明凭证未被撤销，验证者仅获知状态结果而无法观察元数据；对比 Verkle 树与稀疏 Merkle 树的路径深度和 KZG 认证成本。

Result: Groth16 证明时间中位数：稀疏 Merkle 为 371.6ms，Verkle 为 2.11s；验证时间分别为 3.70ms 和 7.55ms；移动端 Verkle 证明约 3s。

Conclusion: 更短的认证路径并不必然带来更便宜的零知识证明；在会话随机性独立假设下，验证者可见状态数据不泄露凭证关联，但该保证不涵盖发行者-验证者共谋与同步流量。

Abstract: Verifiable credentials let holders present digitally signed claims without requiring the issuer to participate in every presentation. Revocation complicates this privacy model because a verifier must determine whether a credential remains valid. Existing status checks may expose recurring identifiers, registry positions, or request metadata. Such information can serve as stable handles to link separate presentations. ShadowPath moves the credential status lookup to the holder. For each presentation, the holder proves, in zero-knowledge, that the credential has not been revoked under the verifier-selected registry root. The verifier learns the status result but not observable metadata. To the best of our knowledge, we provide the first evaluation of Verkle trees for credential revocation and compare them with sparse Merkle trees to assess their applicability in real world applications. The comparison tests whether reducing path depth with Verkle trees offsets the higher cost of KZG-based authentication. Across 30 desktop trials, median Groth16 proving took 371.6ms with sparse Merkle and 2.11s with Verkle. Verification took 3.70ms and 7.55ms, respectively. Groth16 Verkle proving took about 3s on both primary mobile devices. The results show that shorter authenticated paths do not necessarily yield cheaper zero-knowledge proofs. With fresh session randomness, verifier-visible status data do not reveal whether two presentations use the same credential under the stated assumption of session-value independence. This guarantee excludes issuer-verifier collusion and synchronization traffic.

</details>


### [79] [Symposium: Trust via Auditable Records for Communities of AI Scientist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19511)
*Dexter Pratt*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: Symposium是一个用于记录小型科研社区中AI代理操作的框架，提供长期不可变的历史记录和可审计的审计轨迹，支持证据引用和信任评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI合作系统缺乏对科研活动中长期历史、证据来源和信任评估的持久记录，导致AI代理难以在共享证据基础上构建可验证的科学论点。

Method: 通过分离社区持久历史与操作代理，捕获结构化声明、细粒度证据引用、假设和证据使用声明，并提供可部署的发布基础设施和提示组件。

Result: 提供了可工作的实现，使社区能快速搭建运行自己的Symposium，记录分析和科学对话形成审计轨迹。

Conclusion: Symposium为快速演进的AI生态系统提供了独立的科学社区历史框架，强化了可追溯性和目的依赖的信任评估能力。

Abstract: Symposium is a formal framework and practical implementation to record the operation of AI agents deployed by small scientific research communities. Symposium provides long-term, immutable histories of agent-driven research activity, leaving auditable trails of analyses, hypotheses, data, and scientific discourse. This shared record of published artifacts enables agents to build on prior work and preserves the evidence researchers and agents need to make purpose-dependent trust assessments. Symposium captures scientific argument, including structured claims, fine-grained evidence citations, assumptions, and explicit declarations of what material may and may not be used as evidence. Symposium differs from AI co-scientist agents or integrated AI research environments; it is a framework that separates a scientific community's durable history from the agents and other systems that operate on that history. It assumes that a community will use diverse AI systems in a rapidly evolving environment. A working implementation of the publication infrastructure, agent prompt components, and documentation are provided to enable users to rapidly set up and run their own Symposium community.

</details>


### [80] [Tracking the Trend in How Speech Synthesizers Deceive People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19959)
*Milan Šalko,Anton Firc,Kamil Malinka,Vojtěch Staněk,Martin Perešini,Filip Pleško,Jakub Reš*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 随着语音合成技术的发展，人工检测深度伪造音频的准确率显著下降，尤其在部分伪造场景下，人类和检测器均表现不佳，且误报率上升，需依靠程序化验证手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期研究显示人类对深度伪造音频有70-80%的检测准确率，但基于旧式合成器，无法反映现代工具的风险，因此需评估人类对最新合成语音的检测能力。

Method: 选取2019、2022和2024年发布的三款语音合成工具，让82名IT专业人员进行检测，并与六种预训练检测器比较，测试完全伪造和部分伪造两种场景。

Result: 完全伪造时F1分数从RTVC和YourTTS的约90%降至ElevenLabs的48%；部分伪造时严格准确率仅9%，且77%的伪造句被误判为真实；同时误报率上升，人类与检测器错误互补。

Conclusion: 人类对现代及部分伪造语音的感知不可靠，且无法准确定位短篡改，需依赖程序验证、来源追踪、水印和分段检测等技术保障音频真实性。

Abstract: Advances in speech synthesis have made deepfake audio highly realistic. Earlier studies reported 70-80% human detection accuracy, but relied primarily on older synthesizers. We compare human detection for three selected voice synthesis tools released in 2019, 2022, and 2024 with 82 IT professionals, and benchmark humans against six pretrained detectors on the same material. For fully synthetic speech (full spoofs), the F1 score drops from about 90% for RTVC and YourTTS to 48% for ElevenLabs, although listeners were explicitly warned that deepfakes were present. For partial spoofing, where only one sentence of an utterance is altered, strict accuracy falls to 9%, and listeners classify the synthetic sentence as bona fide 77% of the time. Humans and detectors fail in complementary ways, and neither reliably localizes short manipulations. Additionally, listeners increasingly mislabel bona fide speech as fake, eroding trust in unmanipulated audio. These findings show that human perception alone is unreliable for the selected modern and partial-spoof conditions and motivate procedural verification, provenance, watermarking, and segment-level detection.

</details>


### [81] [Trustworthy mobile edge caching: a blockchain approach to mitigate malicious nodes and incentivize cache sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20145)
*Motahare Ebrahimi,Nastooh Taheri Javan,Seyedakbar Mostafavi,Fatemeh Pakzaban*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出基于区块链的信任管理机制，结合智能合约计算直接与间接信任，并引入奖励机制激励缓存共享，以解决边缘缓存中的恶意节点和自私行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘服务器缓存面临容量有限和恶意篡改风险，而辅助移动节点虽能扩容但存在自私和恶意行为，需可靠信任管理与激励机制。

Method: 通过智能合约计算直接和间接信任值，基于服务质量满意度评估可信度，将信任数据存于区块链；设计奖励机制对抗自私，并用区块链认证防止节点冒充，同时考虑移动性、能耗和计算约束。

Result: 仿真证明在数据含10%噪声时仍能准确区分诚实与恶意服务器。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升边缘缓存系统的安全性和协作效率，平衡信任、容量与成本。

Abstract: As mobile network traffic continues to grow, content caching on edge servers is critical for reducing latency. However, challenges such as malicious edge servers that may delete or manipulate cached content, along with the limited capacity of these servers, need to be addressed. To overcome the capacity limitations, helper mobile nodes can contribute their cache resources. However, due to their selfish behavior, an incentive mechanism is necessary to encourage resource sharing. Additionally, these helper nodes can also be malicious. This paper proposes a blockchain-based trust management mechanism that addresses these challenges by accurately identifying trustworthy edge servers and mobile nodes. The proposed mechanism calculates both direct and indirect trust using smart contracts, ensuring that malicious nodes are effectively filtered out. Trustworthiness is determined based on mobile node satisfaction with the quality of service, and trust data is securely stored on the blockchain. To combat node selfishness, a reward mechanism is introduced to incentivize cache sharing. Furthermore, a blockchain-based authentication mechanism protects against node impersonation. Our approach optimizes trust, cache capacity, and cost efficiency while considering mobile node mobility, energy consumption, and computational power constraints during the consensus process. Simulation results show that the proposed method can accurately distinguish between honest and malicious servers, even with a 10% noise in data.

</details>


### [82] [DraftFM: A FoundationModel for Day-Zero Drafting in Magic: The Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19568)
*Brian Ward*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文研究《万智牌》新系列在无历史选牌数据时的“第零天”预测问题，提出DraftFM模型，仅基于卡牌公开文本与结构化特征预测选牌，在完全未见的系列上达到50%以上Top-1准确率，并成功预测未发布系列《霍比特人》的卡牌强度排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督选牌模型依赖已观察的轮抽日志，但新系列发布时这些数据不存在，导致模型无法直接应用。作者希望解决这一“day-zero”冷启动问题，即仅凭公开卡牌信息即可对新系列进行合理选牌预测。

Method: DraftFM采用离散选择策略，对当前卡包内每张卡牌独立打分，条件为已选牌池与轮抽状态。每张卡牌表示为775维固定特征（卡牌公开记录、结构化特征、固定文本嵌入），不使用卡牌身份、系列身份或使用统计，因此对未见卡牌与熟悉卡牌使用同一机制。模型仅160万参数，在1490万人类选牌数据上训练。

Result: 在完全 withheld 的三个系列上，Top-1选牌准确率分别为50.8%、60.4%、56.7%（首轮均匀随机约7%）。重训于全部32个系列后，对未发布系列《霍比特人》生成卡牌排名，与六位独立专家评审的一致性接近专家间一致性。

Conclusion: 作者证明仅基于卡牌文本和结构化特征，无需卡牌ID或历史使用统计，即可在完全未见的新系列上达到与人类专家接近的预测性能。该方法为“第零天”轮抽预测提供可行方案，未来将公布与实际游戏结果的对照评估。

Abstract: Drafting a new Magic: The Gathering expansion begins before any pick from it has been observed: the complete card list is public, but the draft logs that supervised pick models train on do not yet exist. We study this day-zero regime directly. DraftFM is a discrete-choice policy that scores exactly the cards available in the current pack, conditioned on the drafted pool and the state of the draft. Every card enters as a frozen 775-dimensional function of its public card record, structured features and a fixed text embedding, with no card identities, set identities, or usage statistics anywhere in the model, so an unseen card is scored by the same machinery as a familiar one. A 1.6-million-parameter network fitted on 149 million human picks from 29 expansions predicts held-out picks in three expansions withheld in their entirety, reaching 50.8%, 60.4%, and 56.7% top-1 agreement, where uniform chance at the opening pick is about 7%. Refitted on all 32 observed expansions, the same architecture produced a card ranking for the then-unreleased set The Hobbit, sealed with its complete cryptographic provenance and published roughly 36 hours before the set became draftable on MTG Arena. The sealed ranking agrees with six independent expert reviewers roughly as much as those reviewers agree with one another. Evaluation against realized outcomes is committed to a follow-on note, whatever it shows.

</details>


### [83] [A Two-Stage Time-Aware Transformer for Short-Horizon AECOPD Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19578)
*Dongyang Wang,Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Haowen Pan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段框架，直接利用家用呼吸机最近七天的原始压力和流量波形，先分类识别高风险患者，再回归预测恶化事件发生前的剩余天数，实现了风险预警和提前时间估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD病情恶化迅速，现有方法依赖间歇性临床变量，存在延迟；家用呼吸机提供近连续波形数据但未被充分利用，且已有方法要么依赖手工特征，要么仅做二分类，无法预测事件时间。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段用分类模型识别高风险患者，第二阶段用回归模型估计距恶化事件的天数，输入为最近七天的原始压力和流量波形，无需手工特征。

Result: Stage 1分类器F1=0.91，Stage 2回归模型RMSE=1.00天，R^2=0.76，整体优于传统基线模型。

Conclusion: 该方法能为临床医生提供早期预警和可操作的提前时间，有助于在严重恶化发生前进行干预。

Abstract: Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) can worsen rapidly, making timely prediction a clinical priority. Most existing machine learning approaches rely on episodically collected clinical variables, introducing delays that limit their practical utility in home monitoring settings. Home ventilators offer a lower-latency alternative, producing a near-continuous record of respiratory status during daily use. However existing ventilator-based approaches either compress the waveform into handcrafted features or focus primarily on binary risk classification, leaving the timing of an impending event unresolved. In this paper, we present a two-stage framework that operates directly on raw pressure and flow waveforms from the most recent seven days of home ventilator use. The first-stage classification model identifies patients at high risk of a severe exacerbation. The second-stage regression model then estimates how many days remain before the event occurs. Our experimental results demonstrate that the two-stage model outperforms traditional baseline models on both risk classification and time-to-event estimation, with our selected Stage 1 classifier achieving F1 = 0.91 and our Stage 2 regression model achieving RMSE = 1.00 days and R^2 = 0.76, giving clinicians both an early warning and actionable lead time before a severe exacerbation occurs.

</details>


### [84] [Kähler landscapes for complex neural network descents and guarantees including a search and destroy of the Calabi-Yau manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19584)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何视角研究复数参数化网络的损失景观，提出了在Kähler信息度量下的自然梯度下降规则，并分析了Calabi-Yau信息流形上的几何性质及其对优化保证的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论对复数参数网络缺乏几何层面的分析，作者希望借助Dolbeault渐近和Kähler几何提供新的优化保证视角。

Method: 利用Wirtinger Hessian构造对数似然势的Kähler信息度量，将自然梯度下降限制在全纯切丛中；在非紧Calabi-Yau流形上定义全局势，通过楔积无处消失全纯形式导出常数行列式条件，并分析低秩度量导致的爆炸效应及负截面曲率对损失景观的影响。

Result: 发现负曲率（特别是负截面曲率）会破坏损失景观，低秩度量在公差内可引发梯度爆炸，并与负Ricci曲率建立联系；在深度学习中，这对应于初始化渐近和网络保证失效的模式。

Conclusion: 几何分析表明复数网络优化受曲率制约，Calabi-Yau信息流形上的不良曲率条件会导致理论保证退化，需在设计和训练中考虑几何约束。

Abstract: We study landscapes for complex-parameterized networks. Our approach is motivated with an information-theoretic manifold perspective of the parameter and via classical optimization guarantees although of complex geometric variety such as through Dolbeault asymptotics. The descent path admits a Kähler information metric under a cross-entropy via the Wirtinger Hessian on the log-likelihood potential. We restrict attention to a descent update rule with natural gradient descent via a differentiated loss scaled by the inverse metric, so the descent path remains in the holomorphic tangent bundle. We emphasize Calabi-Yau information manifolds which profane theoretical guarantees via an ill-curvature-conditioned landscape. Under a Calabi-Yau metric, specifically in a non-compact setting with a global potential so defined geometrically rather than invoking the topological requirements of the Calabi conjecture, a wedged nowhere-vanishing holomorphic form is the top exterior product of the Kähler form up to constants, yielding a constant determinant condition. Under a fixed determinant, a metric almost low rank up to an eigenvalue tolerance implies a blow-up effect. Moreover, it has been discovered that negative curvature subverts the loss landscape, specifically sectional curvature, so we expand on this and draw interconnections to negative-definite Ricci curvature. Our arguments primarily exist in a geometric analytic modality, although we establish roots in deep learning theory such as through asymptotics at initialization and connections through failure modes of neural network guarantees under vanishing and negative Ricci curvature.

</details>


### [85] [GenMatch: An End-to-End Generative Matching Framework for Micro-View Order-Dispatching in Ride-Hailing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19751)
*Chuang Liu,Yuxueqing Zhang,Tengfei Lyu,Zirui Yuan,Weiqi Hu,Yanghan Cheng,Ming Wang,Li Ma,Zihao Lu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出端到端生成式匹配框架GenMatch，解决网约车微观订单派发中多阶段目标不一致问题，并在滴滴国际业务中落地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业方案采用预测-估值-匹配多阶段范式，各阶段优化目标与最终派发质量不一致，单阶段改进不保证整体提升。

Method: 将派单建模为生成式匹配问题，设计上下文感知二分图编码器、业务感知效用学习器和状态感知指针解码器，分别处理动态稀疏图编码、异构反馈效用学习及匹配状态追踪。

Result: 离线评估和五个城市在线A/B测试均持续优于强基线。

Conclusion: GenMatch是首个落地生产的生成式派单框架，验证了端到端生成建模在工业派单中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Micro-View Order-Dispatching assigns available drivers to passenger orders within each dispatch batch and is critical to the service quality and operational efficiency of ride-hailing platforms. Mainstream industrial solutions follow a multi-stage paradigm of model prediction, value calculation, and dispatch matching. Although dispatch quality is determined by the final batch-level assignment, these stages optimize different intermediate objectives. This cross-stage objective inconsistency means that improving a single stage does not necessarily improve the overall dispatch result. We therefore formulate Micro-View Order-Dispatching as a generative matching problem and propose GenMatch, an end-to-end Generative Matching framework and the first such framework deployed in a real-world production environment. Applying generative modeling to this problem introduces three challenges. First, each dispatch batch forms a dynamic sparse bipartite graph, requiring efficient structured batch-level encoding. Second, replacing the hand-crafted value function requires learning unified business utility from heterogeneous feedback. Third, directly generating an assignment requires tracking the evolving matching state because each selected order-driver pair changes the remaining feasible candidates. GenMatch addresses these challenges with a Context-Aware Bipartite Encoder, a Business-Aware Utility Learner, and a State-Aware Pointer Decoder. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests in five cities across DiDi's international ride-hailing markets show consistent improvements over competitive baselines, confirming the effectiveness and practicality of GenMatch for industrial order-dispatching.

</details>


### [86] [Time-Uniform Self-Normalized Concentration for Discounted Least Squares: Limits and Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19643)
*Yi-Shan Wu*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文指出强化学习/在线学习中广泛使用的加权自归一化集中不等式存在证明错误，其宣称的折扣最小二乘估计的时间一致置信半径在固定参数简单高斯反例下以概率1被穿越；并给出了下界与修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自归一化集中不等式是bandit与强化学习分析的标准工具，加权版本被广泛用于非平稳问题的折扣最小二乘估计，但原始证明存在缺陷，需纠正以避免下游分析失效。

Method: 通过构造固定参数的标量高斯反例展示原不等式的半径被穿越；利用概率论论证对任意确定性时间一致边界在条件次高斯模型类上给出下界；定位证明中不同终端时间使用不同高斯混合分布导致无法形成单一超鞅的问题；最后给出有限与无限时域的有效修正。

Result: 固定折扣与正则化参数下，当δ≤1/2且T/δ足够大时，任意确定性时间一致边界至少在某时刻达到R√log(T/δ)量级；非降边界在时间T即需此量级。原不等式仅在每个固定确定性时刻成立。

Conclusion: 原加权自归一化不等式的时间一致版本不成立，需使用修正版本；这对非平稳bandit/RL的置信集构造与后悔分析有重要影响。

Abstract: Self-normalized concentration inequalities are standard tools in bandit and reinforcement-learning analyses. A widely used weighted extension claims an analogous time-uniform guarantee for discounted least-squares estimators in non-stationary problems. A simple scalar Gaussian counterexample with a fixed parameter shows that the claimed bounded radius is crossed with probability one. For fixed discount and regularization parameters, we further show that, when $δ\leq1/2$ and $T/δ$ is sufficiently large, any deterministic anytime boundary valid uniformly over the stated conditionally sub-Gaussian model class must be at least of order $R\sqrt{\log(T/δ)}$ at some time by horizon $T$; for nondecreasing boundaries, this order is required at time $T$. We identify the proof error: different terminal times use different Gaussian mixing distributions, so the fixed-time mixtures do not form one supermartingale, and the stopping-time argument does not repair this failure. Finally, we show that the weighted inequality remains valid at each fixed deterministic time, give valid finite- and infinite-horizon corrections, and discuss consequences for downstream analyses.

</details>


### [87] [SAGE-XGBoost: Spatially Augmented Graph Embeddings--Machine Learning Framework for Natural Hazards Susceptibility Mapping under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19672)
*Mohammad H. Vahidnia,Ali Pourkarimi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，通过噪声增强和图嵌入提升数据稀缺下的自然灾害易发性预测，精度显著优于传统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然灾害易发性制图常受限于标注数据稀缺，传统机器学习泛化能力不足，深度学习又难以应用。

Method: 构建K近邻图提取局部空间统计量，经PCA降维后与环境协变量及坐标集成，用XGBoost训练SAGE-XGBoost模型。

Result: 在滑坡和野火案例中，AUC分别约0.97和0.95，比Spatial XGBoost绝对提升超33个百分点。

Conclusion: SAGE-XGBoost是高效、可迁移的浅层替代方案，适用于少监督条件下的地理空间预测任务。

Abstract: Natural hazard susceptibility mapping is often constrained by limited labeled data, reducing the generalizability of conventional machine learning and limiting the applicability of complex deep learning models. This study proposes SAGE (Spatially Augmented Graph Embeddings), a structurally informed feature-engineering framework that combines controlled noise-based data augmentation with neighborhood-based graph embeddings to improve prediction under data-scarce conditions. A K-nearest neighbor graph is constructed to derive local spatial statistics, which are reduced using principal component analysis and integrated with environmental covariates and spatial coordinates. The resulting features are used with XGBoost to develop the SAGE-XGBoost model. The framework was evaluated for landslide and wildfire susceptibility mapping. SAGE-XGBoost consistently outperformed conventional and spatially explicit machine learning models. Compared with Spatial XGBoost, it achieved an absolute improvement of above 33 percentage points across the two case studies. The model reached AUC values of approximately 0.97 for landslide susceptibility and 0.95 for wildfire susceptibility. Feature importance analysis confirmed the contribution of graph embeddings to prediction, while their integration improved spatial coherence and reduced local noise amplification. Overall, SAGE-XGBoost provides an efficient and transferable alternative to deep representation learning for environmental hazard assessment and other geospatial prediction tasks under limited supervision.

</details>


### [88] [Rethinking Patch Based Multivariate Time Series Forecasting with Semantic Structured Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19966)
*Jiazhe Wang,Zhiquan Huang,Linjing Xue,Ming Liu,Meiwen Li,Ruijuan Zheng*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出SCPaT，一种基于语义结构化分区的Transformer框架，通过自适应语义单元生成、动态语义图和重要性感知路由机制，解决多变量时间序列预测中现有分区方法的局限性，并在12个数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于patch的预测方法存在固定分区破坏语义边界、多尺度分区引入冗余、扩展分区缺乏语义结构组织及异质时序模式交互建模机制的问题。

Method: SCPaT包含三个核心组件：自适应语义单元生成（将输入序列分解为语义一致的单元）、动态语义图（建模单元间有向依赖并组织成高阶语义块）、重要性感知路由机制（将不同语义块分配给不同专家进行定制化建模）。

Result: 在12个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明SCPaT方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SCPaT通过语义结构分区和动态路由，有效提升了多变量时间序列预测性能，为现有分区方法提供了一种替代方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting (MTSF) is a fundamental task in many real world applications. Existing patch based forecasting methods generally fall into three categories: fixed partitioning, multi-scale partitioning, and extendable partitioning. Fixed partitioning often breaks meaningful temporal boundaries, multi-scale partitioning may introduce redundant representations across scales, and extendable partitioning improves flexibility but still lacks an explicit mechanism for organizing semantic structure and modeling interactions among heterogeneous temporal patterns. To address these limitations, we propose SCPaT, a Transformer based framework built on semantic structured partitioning. SCPaT first decomposes input sequences into semantically consistent units through adaptive semantic unit generation, then constructs a dynamic semantic graph to model directed dependencies among these units and organize them into higher order semantic blocks. Based on these structured representations, an importance aware routing mechanism adaptively dispatches different semantic blocks to different experts for customized modeling. Extensive experiments on 12 real world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SCPaT.

</details>


### [89] [ExPhy: A Benchmark for Explicit Physical Property Learning in Multi-Object Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20009)
*Rui Wang,Yeteng Wu,Xianlin Zhang,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本文提出ExPhy基准，包含24,000个多物体物理场景及显式物理属性标签，并引入PhyODE模型，在长时程OOD-Initial设置下相比最强基线显著降低ADE和FDE，同时揭示轨迹预测精度与物理属性恢复准确性并不完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在轨迹预测中很少将物体级物理属性（如质量、摩擦、恢复系数）作为显式评估目标，导致模型难以被全面检验其对物理规律的真实理解。

Method: 构建ExPhy基准，包含ID、OOD-Parameter和OOD-Initial三种数据划分；提出PhyODE，一种具有显式属性接口的物理引导模型，先从观测轨迹估计物理属性，再通过可微分的未来滚动预测利用这些属性。

Result: 在长时程OOD-Initial设置下，PhyODE相比最强基线的ADE降低33.1%，FDE降低31.0%；零样本迁移到ComPhy评估；属性级分析表明轨迹预测准确并不保证物理属性恢复准确。

Conclusion: ExPhy和PhyODE为联合评估轨迹预测与物理属性估计提供了有效工具，并强调了物理属性恢复作为独立评估维度的必要性。

Abstract: Understanding object dynamics requires not only predicting future trajectories but also examining whether a model captures the physical properties that govern motion. However, existing benchmarks rarely expose object-level physical properties as explicit evaluation targets alongside trajectory forecasting. To address this gap, we introduce \emph{ExPhy}, a multi-object trajectory forecasting benchmark containing 24,000 simulated physical scenes with explicit object-level labels for mass, friction, and restitution. ExPhy provides observed and future trajectories together with an in-distribution (ID) split and two out-of-distribution (OOD) splits over physical parameters (OOD-Parameter) and initial states (OOD-Initial) for jointly evaluating trajectory forecasting and physical property estimation. We further instantiate \textsc{PhyODE}, a physics-guided model with an explicit property interface that estimates physical properties from observed trajectories and uses them for differentiable future rollout. On the long-horizon OOD-Initial setting, \textsc{PhyODE} reduces ADE and FDE by 33.1\% and 31.0\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Zero-shot evaluation on ComPhy further assesses cross-benchmark transfer. Property-level analyses reveal that accurate trajectory forecasting does not necessarily imply accurate recovery of the underlying physical properties. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Zest86/ExPhy.

</details>


### [90] [Reliable Neural Collapse Approximation for Open-World Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19890)
*Jia-Qi Lin,Yuangang Pan,Chang-Dong Wang,Haizhang Zhang,Ivor W. Tsang,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出ReNC方法，利用神经坍缩结构先验，通过原型过滤OOD样本并逐步精炼原型，实现开放世界测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TTA方法在标签分布偏移（开放世界场景）下失效，需要可靠的OOD过滤和原型适应机制。

Method: 以神经坍缩为结构先验，用预训练分类器权重作为源域原型，通过样本-原型相似度过滤OOD样本，并设计神经坍缩近似机制逐步精炼原型以适应目标域。

Result: 在多个开放世界基准上优于现有方法，更好地保持目标域中神经坍缩相关性质。

Conclusion: ReNC提供了可靠的OWTTA解释和新模型设计思路，代码已公开。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods aim to bridge the domain gap between the source and target domains. However, traditional TTA methods become ineffective when the label distribution shift occurs, a challenge commonly referred to as an open-world scenario. In this paper, we introduce a new method named Reliable Neural Collapse approximation (ReNC) for Open-World Test-Time Adaptation (OWTTA). Specifically, we leverage neural collapse as a structural prior for reliable target-domain adaptation. Guided by this prior, we justify that the pre-trained classifier weights can serve as the prototypes of the source domain. By measuring the similarity between samples and prototypes, we filter out the Out-Of-Distribution~(OOD) samples for reliable updates. Furthermore, we propose a neural collapse approximation mechanism to refine these prototypes, ensuring they can gradually adapt to the target domain while maintaining the neural collapse structure. Extensive experiments on several open-world benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method. Our empirical analysis suggests that ReNC better preserves NC-related properties in the target domain, providing useful evidence for explaining reliable OWTTA and offering new insights for model design. Code is available at https://github.com/JiaqiLin-AI/ReNC.

</details>


### [91] [Auditing Recorded Predictive Lead Service-Line Classifications Against Physical Verification: A Statewide Study of New York](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19922)
*Muhammad Sarmad Sohail*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 纽约州有49%的城镇在服务管线材料记录中仅有一个值，其中7个被自身验证数据反驳，纽约市最大案例中模型预测的43,215个地址全部记录为“已知其他”，与全州其他地区12.21%的铅检出率形成鲜明对比，估计模型清除人群中有1,150-1,450条铅管被遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依据美国铅铜规则修订版，公用事业公司可使用预测模型而非实际检查来确定服务管线材料。纽约州公开记录中几乎没有地址同时具有模型分类和物理验证，因此需要在人群层面而非配对地址层面进行核查，以评估模型是否系统性低估铅管存在。

Method: 筛选纽约州153个至少有100个地址被分类的地区，检查记录值是否单一；对单一值地区与其自身验证数据比对；使用六种考虑建筑年代（年份中位数、建造时期固定效应）的估计方法，推算预期铅管数量；并检查2025年快照中公共侧判定是否复制自客户侧模型输出。

Result: 75个地区（49%）记录单一值，其中68个无可疑，7个被自身工作人员数据反驳，且无法用抽样解释；纽约市5个区作为单一系统，模型记录的43,215个地址全部为“已知其他”，铅检出率95%置信上限为0.0085%，而全州其他地区相同方法记录的铅或“未知但可能含铅”比例为12.21%；模型清除人群建筑年代中位数为1984年（对照1930年），固定建造时期后模型仍无铅检出，但物理验证率为1.5-14.5%，实际验证率为4.3-31.9%。

Conclusion: 纽约市的模型预测极不可能正确，预估计有1,150-1,450条铅管被误分类为“已知其他”；此外，7,782个地址位于1940年前建筑中，且存在公共侧判定复制自客户侧模型输出的证据，表明模型存在系统性缺陷和错误复制，亟需物理验证和监管纠正。

Abstract: Under the US Lead and Copper Rule Revisions, a utility may determine a service line's material with a predictive model instead of inspecting it. New York State publishes, per address, which method was used. Almost no address carries both a model classification and a physical verification, so the check is between populations within a utility rather than paired addresses. We screen all 153 New York localities that classified at least 100 addresses this way. Seventy-five (49%), covering 125,990 addresses or 57% of those screened, record one value. Zero variance alone is not misconduct: 68 of the 75 match their own verification or have too little to test. Seven are contradicted by their own crews, six beyond any sampling explanation. Five are boroughs of New York City, which file as one system; one is East Rochester, 550 km away. New York City is the largest case: a predictive model is the recorded basis for 43,215 addresses, and on all of them the recorded material is "Known Other". The city records "Unknown" on 121,779 addresses, 1,880 already excavated, and lead on 120,692. In the model bucket both counts are zero, and the 95% upper bound on the rate is 0.0085%. Across the rest of New York the same method records lead or the hedge "Unknown but could be lead" on 12.21% of 176,888 addresses, a comparison whose weaknesses we report. The model-cleared population is newer, median year built 1984 against 1930, and construction era accounts for about a third of the gap and not the rest: holding era fixed, records-based classification finds lead at 4.3-31.9%, physical verification at 1.5-14.5%, the model in no era. Six era-aware estimators place the expected lead lines among them at 1,150-1,450. Two findings need no comparison: 7,782 of these addresses are in pre-1940 buildings, and the archived 2025 snapshot shows the public-side determination was copied from a customer-side model output.

</details>


### [92] [CLaST: Context-aware Contrastive VAE for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20025)
*Alexander Marusov,Dmitry Anikin,Petr Sokerin,Vitaliy Pozdnyakov,Ilya Kuleshov,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出CLaST框架，通过对比损失增强VAE的时序依赖建模，在9个基准上超越现有方法，短期CRPS提升16.4%，长期提升48.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型在概率时间序列预测中难以捕捉内部时间依赖，导致潜在表示表达能力有限。

Method: 在VAE框架中引入对比损失，学习保持观测间上下文相似性的嵌入表示。

Result: 在9个基准上全面超越强基线；短期CRPS最高提升16.4%、NMAE提升14.4%；长期CRPS最高提升48.6%、NMAE提升25.1%。

Conclusion: CLaST通过对比学习增强潜在表示的时序表达能力，显著提升概率多变量时间序列预测性能。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting models are widely used for time series forecasting in domains such as energy systems, finance, medicine, and transportation. In recent years, deep generative models have shown strong results on probabilistic forecasting, yet many conventional approaches struggle to capture internal temporal dependencies, leading to latent representations with limited expressive power. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{CLaST}, a VAE framework for probabilistic multivariate time series forecasting. Unlike existing generative models, CLaST learns embeddings that preserve contextual similarity between observations through our contrastive loss function. Experiments across nine widely adopted benchmarks demonstrate that CLaST consistently surpasses strong baseline methods. In short-term forecasting tasks, our approach achieves improvements of up to $16.4\%$ in CRPS and $14.4\%$ in NMAE over the second-best method. Furthermore, in long-term prediction CLaST attains superior overall performance, exceeding the second-best method by up to $48.6\%$ and $25.1\%$ in CRPS and NMAE, respectively.

</details>


### [93] [InsufficiencyBench: Evaluating LLM legal advice on underspecified user queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20220)
*Samuel J. Vincent,Daniel Calloway,Fangyi Yu,Andrew M. Bean,Nabeel Seedat*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 首个面向法律问答查询侧信息缺失的基准InsuffiencyBench，发现现有前沿模型在识别缺失要素上F2最高仅0.46，且无法平衡拒答与直接回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI基准假设查询信息完整，但实际用户常遗漏决定法律结果的关键事实，导致模型在信息不足时给出误导性答案，缺乏针对查询侧不充分性的系统评测。

Method: 构建八类缺失要素分类体系和三种结构失效模式（切换、门控、致命前提），由执业律师标注202个测试项（58个基础查询+144个缺陷变体），覆盖6个法律领域和24个美国司法辖区，并评估10个前沿模型。

Result: 所有模型在缺失要素识别上F2≤0.46，中位召回率0.44；模型要么无差别模糊回应，要么在未明示假设下直接作答，无一能在缺陷查询上同时做到识别与限定回答，同时正确处理完整查询。

Conclusion: 当前法律AI在查询不充分场景下性能显著不足，急需开发能主动识别缺失信息并避免过早结论的模型，基准为后续改进提供了标准化评测基础。

Abstract: Legal AI systems are increasingly used to answer legal questions, yet existing benchmarks assume queries arrive fully specified. In practice, users omit facts that materially determine the legal outcome. We introduce InsufficiencyBench, the first legal benchmark targeting query-side insufficiency: whether a model recognizes when a query lacks legally material information, identifies what is missing, and refrains from premature conclusions. We formalize a taxonomy of eight canonical missing-element categories across three structural failure modes---switch, gating, and fatal prerequisite--- and construct 202 benchmark items (58 base queries, 144 deficient variants) spanning six legal domains and 24 US jurisdictions and annotated by practising attorneys. Evaluating ten frontier models, we find that no model exceeds F2 = 0.46 on missing-element identification and that the median recall is 0.44. Models either hedge indiscriminately or answer silently under fabricated presumptions. No model both identifies and qualifies responses to deficient queries while directly addressing complete ones.

</details>


### [94] [Exact Algebraic Computation of Learning Coefficients for Two-Dimensional Singular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20183)
*Grégoire Sergeant-Perthuis,Elias Tsigaridas,Jules Tsukahara*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出首个确定性算法，可精确计算任意二维奇异模型的局部RLCT，并证明其复杂度上界，在多项式神经网络等模型中验证有效性，为采样估计提供基准且速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典信息准则如BIC在奇异模型（如深度学习）中失效，而WBIC依赖的局部学习系数精确计算长期受限，现有方法多为采样估计，缺乏确定性算法和代数结构理解。

Method: 基于Kullback-Leibler距离与多项式的接触等价性，设计确定性算法计算二维模型的局部RLCT，并推导复杂度上界，通过多项式神经网络等实例验证。

Result: 算法精确计算局部RLCT，速度快于采样估计，能揭示采样方法无法获得的代数结构，并在浅层模型中表现出效率优势。

Conclusion: 该算法填补了奇异模型学习系数精确计算的空白，为校准采样估计提供理论基准，并拓展了对学习系数代数结构的理解。

Abstract: Classical information criteria such as the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC) rely on regularity assumptions that break down for singular models, leading to incorrect model selection in settings such as deep learning. The Widely Applicable Bayesian Information Criterion (WBIC) relies on local learning coefficients $λ$, which in the analytic case coincides with local Real Log Canonical Thresholds (RLCT) of the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the model, to capture correct marginal likelihood asymptotics. Exact computation of the learning coefficients has been limited to special cases, and only sampling-based estimation methods are generally applicable. We present the first deterministic algorithm that computes local RLCTs exactly for any two-dimensional model whose Kullback-Leibler distance is contact equivalent to a polynomial, derive a bound on its complexity, and demonstrate its effectiveness for a broad class of models, with applications including polynomial neural networks. Beyond providing ground truth to calibrate sampling-based estimators, exact computation reveals algebraic structure in learning coefficients that sampling cannot and out-speeds it in the shallow regime.

</details>


### [95] [Dynamic Structural Causal Modeling for Sleep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20285)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Pranuthi Tenali,Arun Badi,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 利用PCMCI+算法从家庭睡眠呼吸暂停测试记录中学习睡眠呼吸障碍的动态因果图，揭示不同性别和年龄亚群间因果结构的系统性差异，同时发现时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有队列中稳定存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠呼吸障碍的因果动态复杂且因患者群体而异，阻碍了针对性干预措施的开发，因此需要识别跨人群的因果结构差异以促进精准治疗。

Method: 基于105份HSAT记录，采用PCMCI+算法对窗口化分数变量进行因果发现，利用领域知识进行边黑名单约束，并通过自助聚合处理小规模亚组样本。

Result: 学习到的因果图显示时间自依赖和呼吸暂停-去饱和关系在所有队列中持续存在，而其他因果连接则表现出显著的跨亚群差异。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效揭示睡眠呼吸障碍中稳定与可变因果结构，为理解人群特异性病理机制及指导个性化干预提供依据。

Abstract: The causal dynamics of sleep-disordered breathing are complex and vary across patient populations, hindering the development of targeted interventions. We learn dynamic causal graphs of sleep-disordered breathing from Home Sleep Apnea Test (HSAT) recordings, revealing systematic differences in causal structure across sex and age subcohorts. We do so using the PCMCI+ algorithm on windowed fractional variables derived from 105 HSAT recordings, exploiting domain knowledge via edge blacklisting and employing bootstrap aggregation to address small subcohort sizes. The learned graphs show that temporal self-dependencies and the apnea-desaturation relationship persist across all cohorts, while other relationships vary substantially.

</details>


### [96] [An Agentic Approach for Active Data Collection, Travel Behavior Modeling, and Weather-Sensitive Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20320)
*Narges Ahmadi,Yubo Jiao,Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Jiangbo Yu,Luis Miranda-Moreno*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种三代理工作流，整合对话式数据收集、结构化数据处理和行为预测，并在天气情景下通过454个观测样本比较了多项Logit、机器学习及九种本地部署的LLM（含视觉变体）的表现，最佳视觉配置达到71.5%的五分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出行行为研究中数据收集与预测建模常被割裂开发与评估，缺乏端到端的协调工作流；同时大语言模型在行为预测中的潜力及其与对话式调查的整合方式尚未被系统探索。

Method: 采用聊天机器人管理的图像增强陈述偏好调查，收集通勤学生五种预设天气下的出行方式选择；基于多项Logit分析天气关联，以Logistic回归和随机森林作为机器学习基准，并评估九种参数规模从2B到35B的本地LLM，涵盖零样本、persona、few-shot及视觉条件，同时测试习惯性出行信息、专家框架等提示变体。

Result: 随机森林五分类准确率为69.6%，最佳纯文本零样本LLM达69.9%；习惯性出行信息带来最稳定的增益，专家框架优于角色扮演，persona在缺乏习惯信息时最有效；few-shot仅小幅提升；视觉配置使用与受访者相同的天气图像后达到71.5%的准确率。

Conclusion: 对话式调查、结构化处理、传统模型、机器学习和多模态LLM可被协调在可审计的多代理工作流中，视觉上下文为选定的模型提供额外预测信息，提示策略对LLM效果影响显著且具有可解释的规律。

Abstract: Travel behavior research increasingly combines digital data collection with predictive modeling, yet these stages are often developed and evaluated separately. This study proposes a three-agent workflow integrating conversational data collection, structured data processing, and behavioral prediction. A chatbot-administered, image-augmented stated-preference survey collected mode choices from student commuters across five predefined weather scenarios, yielding 454 respondent-scenario observations. Weather-related associations were analyzed using a multinomial logit model, while logistic regression and random forest provided machine-learning benchmarks. Nine locally deployed large language models (LLMs), ranging from 2 to 35 billion parameters, were evaluated across four zero-shot prompt-and-context conditions and extended through persona, few-shot, and vision-based configurations. Random forest achieved 69.6% five-class accuracy, while the best text-only zero-shot LLM reached 69.9% without task-specific fitting. Habitual travel information produced the most consistent gains, Expert framing generally outperformed Role-Play, and persona information was most useful when habitual travel information was unavailable. Few-shot prompting improved prediction for several models, with gains stabilizing after a small number of examples. Using the same weather images shown to respondents, the best vision-based configuration reached 71.5% five-class accuracy, indicating that visual context may provide additional predictive information for selected models. Overall, the study shows how conversational surveys, structured data processing, conventional behavioral modeling, machine learning, and multimodal LLM prediction can be coordinated within an auditable multi-agent workflow.

</details>


### [97] [Explainable Transformer Models for Clinical Prediction Tasks on Structured Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20315)
*Jun Ni Du,Lukas Adamek,Maxim Kryukov,Flavio Dormont,Ziv Bar-Joseph,Sven Jager,Brandon Rufino*

Main category: Model

TL;DR: 提出BERT-LER，一种基于BERT的编码EHR时间线模型，通过百分位分箱将实验室检验结果编码为离散token，并集成Integrated Gradients实现可解释性，在75M患者数据上预训练，在EHRShot和哮喘进展任务上达到竞争性性能且归因与临床风险因素一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR预测模型很少同时强调定量实验室信息与对输入医疗事件的可解释性，存在方法学空白。

Method: 采用BERT-style架构，将实验室检验结果按百分位分箱转为离散token，保留分级信息；使用Integrated Gradients为输入token提供归因，并基于去标识化的7500万患者EHR数据预训练和微调。

Result: 在EHRShot基准和哮喘严重度进展任务中，性能与公开基准模型竞争，且在实验室相关任务上常超越；归因与已知临床风险因素一致。

Conclusion: 该架构与可解释方法可推广至多种治疗领域和预测任务，适用于基于结构化EHR训练的语言模型。

Abstract: Predictive models over structured electronic health records (EHRs) remain central to machine learning for healthcare, but few have jointly emphasized quantitative laboratory information and interpretability with respect to input medical events. We present BERT-LER, a BERT-style model for coded EHR timelines pretrained and fine-tuned from a de-identified EHR dataset of 75 million patients, that encodes laboratory test results as discrete tokens while retaining graded information through percentile-based binning, paired with Integrated Gradients for token-level attributions grounded in the input EHR sequence. We benchmark our approach on the public EHRShot benchmark suite and on an asthma severity progression study based on real-world data. This addresses a methodological gap in EHR foundation-style modeling by unifying laboratory value representation and explainability in a single framework, while assessing whether both predictive performance and explanations generalize beyond standard clinical prediction tasks. Across EHRShot and asthma tasks, BERT-LER achieves predictive performance that is competitive with, and on laboratory-related tasks often exceeds, publicly available benchmark models, and provides attributions that align with clinically known risk factors. Our architecture and explainability approach can be applied to many therapeutic areas and prediction tasks using language models trained on structured EHRs.

</details>

### [98] [Towards On-Board Implementation of ML-Based Helicopter Weight Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19210)
*Nicolas Valot,Ammar Mechouche,Benjamin Lesage,Claire Pagetti,Louis Fabre*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于长短期记忆递归神经网络的直升机起飞重量估计模型，并遵循EASA和EUROCAE标准进行了学习保证，最终在传统航电计算机上验证了其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直升机起飞重量估计对于飞行安全和性能计算至关重要，传统方法可能不精确或依赖人工计算，且航空领域对机器学习模型的适航认证有严格要求，因此需要一种符合监管框架的监督学习模型。

Method: 利用空客全球在役机队的大规模数据集，开发了长短期记忆递归神经网络模型，并提出了机器学习需求、模型描述及实施，同时遵循EASA概念文件和EUROCAE ED-324标准进行学习保证流程。

Result: 在传统航电计算机上成功实施了该模型，并验证了需求与实现的一致性，证明其可用于机载关键功能如起飞警报。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了符合航空认证要求的机器学习模型在机载环境中的可行性，为直升机重量估计提供了可靠且安全的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the implementation of a novel supervised Machine Learning model for estimating helicopter weight during takeoff, utilizing extensive datasets from Airbus's global in-service fleet. The study details a learning assurance process aligned with the EASA concept paper for machine learning application, and with the on-going Eurocae ED-324. We propose a set of Machine Learning Requirements, a Machine Learning Model Description, and its implementation for a long short-term memory recurrent neural network. Finally, we verify the requirements on the implementation. Demonstrated on legacy avionics computers, the implementation is suitable for the deployment of the developed Machine Learning Model weight estimator on airborne targets for critical functions such as on-board alerting.

</details>


### [99] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文获取建模为主动推断问题，通过内层信念更新和外层决策选择澄清、检索或任务动作，以最小化期望自由能，并在确定性环境下简化为信息增益与token成本的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI代理需高效获取上下文，但用户遗漏约束时，代理面临默认假设或消耗token澄清/检索的权衡，现有方法缺乏统一理论框架。

Method: 构建双层主动推断框架：内层更新潜在任务状态的后验，外层选择上下文动作、任务动作或停止，以最小化期望自由能；确定性下用预期信息增益（按token成本归一化）近似。实例化为最优提问（OQA），采用精确后验和动态规划，并用基准前沿语言模型测试25-300候选的二元/多元分类任务。

Result: 在二元和多元分类任务中验证了OQA的有效性，并研究了生成前澄清和token预算下的自动提示优化。

Conclusion: 该框架模型无关，将主动推断作为AI代理上下文获取层的设计原则，为高效交互提供理论指导。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [100] [A Federated Learning Framework for Privacy-Preserving Oral Cancer Screening on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19462)
*Lena D. Swamikannan,Akshay Bhagwan Sonawane,Jay S. Patel,C. S. Mani,Lakshmi Narayana,Lakshman Tamil*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于联邦学习的分布式口腔癌筛查框架，采用Tailscale安全组网和Flower聚合，轻量级模型MNv4-Conv-S在全局模型中取得最佳性能（AUC 0.929，准确率87%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗影像数据获取受隐私法规限制，口腔图像尤其稀缺，联邦学习可在不共享数据的前提下训练稳健模型，但实际分布式部署框架尚缺验证。

Method: 利用Tailscale构建跨地域安全网络，Flower框架实现FedAvg聚合；手动配置客户端，评估MobileNetV2/V3Large/V4-Conv-S等轻量架构用于智能手机端筛查。

Result: 全局模型GM-V4（基于MNv4-Conv-S）表现最优，AUC达0.929，准确率87%，验证了轻量模型在联邦场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架无需企业级FL平台即可实现多中心协作，为隐私受限场景下的医疗AI部署提供可行方案，未来可扩展至更广泛疾病筛查。

Abstract: Data are the cornerstone of robust AI models. However, in the medical domain, access to reliable data is constrained by regulatory requirements and patient privacy, and clinical oral images are particularly difficult to obtain. Federated learning (FL) mitigates these constraints by enabling collaborative model development across decentralized datasets without centralizing or sharing patient data.
  This work presents a practical FL framework that supports geographically distributed collaboration among AI healthcare researchers and facilitates the development of robust models for oral cancer screening. Client devices were interconnected via Tailscale to provide secure networking and real-time communication. We implemented the FL workflow using the Flower framework for server-side aggregation, while client deployment and orchestration were configured manually; no enterprise FL platforms were used. To support a smartphone-based screening application, we evaluated lightweight, mobile-friendly architectures including MobileNetV2, MobileNetV3Large, and MobileNetV4-Conv-Small (MNv4-Conv-S). Across the global lightweight models aggregated using FedAvg, the MNv4-Conv-S based global model (GM-V4) achieved the best performance, reaching an AUC of 0.929 and an accuracy of 87%

</details>


### [101] [Holtercare-Bench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Dynamic ECG Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19297)
*Yihan Xie,Hanwen Cui,Runze Ye,Juekai Lin,Haoyang Wang,Jinhao Mao,Bo Zhang,Wenqiao Zhang,Xiaogang Guo,Jun Xiao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出 Holtercare-23K 动态心电数据集与 Holtercare-Bench 基准，揭示现有 MLLM 在超长病理序列处理上的不足，并证明微调可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 多聚焦静态图像或短期信号，在动态心电图中缺乏复杂时间推理与报告生成能力，且缺少高质量数据集与基准。

Method: 构建包含 22,980 个 QA 对的 Holtercare-23K 数据集，采用信号-视频-文本三模态对齐；并设计 Holtercare-Bench 基准，评估时间定位、临床诊断与全局总结能力。

Result: 零样本评估显示主流 MLLM 在处理超长病理序列时性能差距显著；对代表模型进行微调后性能大幅提升。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了当前 MLLM 在心电生理学中的局限性，并为长期医疗 MLLM 提供了基础性基准。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel in medical applications, most of them favor static images or short-term signals. In the critical field of dynamic electrocardiograms (ECG), models struggle with complex temporal reasoning and diagnostic report generation due to a lack of high-quality datasets and benchmarks. To address this, we introduce (i) Holtercare-23K, a large-scale multimodal dynamic ECG dataset comprising 22,980 QA pairs derived from 788 clinical Holter records and featuring a novel signal-video-text tri-modal alignment. Based on this dataset, we present (ii) Holtercare-Bench, a multimodal benchmark that evaluates models on temporal localization, clinical diagnosis, and global summarization. Zero-shot evaluations of leading MLLMs reveal a significant performance gap in processing ultra-long pathological sequences. However, fine-tuning representative models yields substantial improvements. This work illuminates the limitations of current MLLMs in electrophysiology and provides a foundational benchmark for long-term medical MLLMs. Our project is available at https://github.com/ZJU4HealthCare/Holtercare-Bench.

</details>


### [102] [Improved Confidence Estimates for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19323)
*Sokhna Diarra Mbacke,Mouloud Belbahri,Gabriel Loaiza-Ganem*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 利用数据集中的标注信息构建简单分类器，以现有UQ分数和相似查询的正确性为特征，显著提升LLM不确定性量化的性能，计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化方法多为零样本且无需标注数据，但在实际部署前仍需在目标数据集上评估其效果；现有方法忽略了这一评估阶段可用的数据信息，导致性能未达最优。

Method: 基于目标数据集的标注，训练简单分类器预测LLM响应正确性；特征包括现有不确定性分数以及相似查询的正确性标签，并在部署时进行推理。

Result: 在多个数据集上一致优于现有零样本UQ分数，且仅产生最小计算开销，适用于现实场景中的高效增强。

Conclusion: 利用部署前的数据集评估环节进行轻量级分类器训练，是一种廉价且直接有效的UQ改进策略，适合实际应用。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). Existing methods, from verbalized confidence to ones requiring multiple generations, are often zero-shot and produce scores quantifying uncertainty without the need for labelled data. Nonetheless, in practice one must always evaluate their performance on a dataset of interest before deployment. In this work we show that, by leveraging this dataset, we consistently outperform these existing scores. Specifically, we build simple classifiers that predict LLM response correctness by using these scores and the correctness of similar queries as features. Our method produces minimal computational overhead, making it a cheap and straightforward enhancement for UQ in LLMs for real-world applications.

</details>


### [103] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于姿态估计的奶牛社交网络分析框架，将视频中的互动行为按效价（亲和/对抗）分层，发现混合计数会掩盖行为结构，需要纵向验证才能用于福利监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化牲畜监测将行为视为孤立事件，忽略了社交关系对资源获取、冲突和群体稳定性的影响，缺乏对互动效价（亲和/对抗）的区分。

Method: 使用基于姿态的计算机视觉管道分析商业奶牛场挤奶前区域7小时39分钟连续视频，经质量控制保留1183个候选互动（36头牛、177个二联组），按预测效价构建网络层，并进行分类平衡审计和网络分析（密度、传递性、社区检测、中心性）。

Result: 审计样本中自动与人工标签一致率为82.8%，未加权宏F1为0.872；聚合网络连通（密度0.281，传递性0.513，平均路径1.88），亲和事件形成5个社区（Q=0.429）；对抗事件占保留事件的72.4%和时长的76.0%；拥有最多伙伴的牛并非介数中心性最高；按效价分层后网络结构明显不同。

Conclusion: 混合互动计数会掩盖网络的行为构成，效价感知分析为检验竞争、亲和和福利相关变化提供框架，但需纵向验证后才能作为福利或健康指标使用。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [104] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Outcome Monitors机制，通过挖掘任务无关轨迹或公共模式中的结果契约来检测工具调用失败，在不阻断执行的情况下提供非约束性恢复建议，显著提升任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用超时可见失败，但缓存错误页或负价格等格式正确但内容错误的结果会被当作事实消费，现有方法难以检测此类语义违规。

Method: 从任务轨迹或公共schema挖掘结果契约，监测输出是否违反契约；违规时保留结果并生成包含违规属性和公开恢复工具的收据，在冻结预评估中注入故障测试。

Result: ToolMaze完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%（四个模型、两个供应商族，并复现于第三个）；tau-bench零售两个层级提升14.0和12.0点；移除恢复工具列表则增益消失，诊断细节和时机无显著差异。检测超出契约词汇的故障准确率降至46%，但完成率不变。

Conclusion: 恢复工具有效性关键，且需扩展检测能力以覆盖非契约词汇，但当前机制已证明简单非阻断干预能显著提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [105] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出R2-OPD方法，通过检测教师奖励与推理进展之间的排名不一致，选择性抑制冲突的蒸馏信号，从而提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线蒸馏（OPD）假设教师奖励能准确反映推理进展，但实际中二者常冲突：推理进展明显的步骤可能因偏离教师输出而获得低奖励，导致模型优化方向偏误。

Method: 构建轨迹内推理片段的两类排名：教师奖励排名与独立估计的进展奖励排名。当两者不一致时抑制相应蒸馏奖励，减少冲突监督，保留有效教师指导。

Result: 在多种推理任务上，R2-OPD相比标准OPD取得一致性能提升，尤其在推理相关指标上表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过过滤与推理进展冲突的教师奖励，可有效缓解监督信号失配问题，提升模型推理能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward. Distillation rewards are selectively suppressed whenever the two rankings disagree, reducing supervision that conflicts with reasoning progress while preserving effective teacher guidance. Our approach shows consistent improvement over standard OPD especially regarding reasoning performances.

</details>


### [106] [LLM as Detector: An In-context Learning Approach for Tabular Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19463)
*Tu Anh Hoang Nguyen,Dang Nguyen,Thuc Duy Le,Trung Le,Sunil Gupta*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Detector框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，将表格数据的统计摘要、因果依赖和原型信息转化为代码生成提示，自动构建评分引擎，无需微调或训练神经网络即可在24个表格数据集上超越15个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据中的异常常表现为跨特征依赖的违背而非简单边际偏差，现有检测器依赖几何或重构信号，而基于LLM的方法需要微调或生成合成异常，缺乏利用LLM上下文学习能力直接进行结构化推理的方案。

Method: 将正常训练数据转化为统计摘要、因果依赖和蒸馏原型，组织成Prompt引导LLM生成评分代码；该评分引擎综合统计偏差、结构不一致性和基于密度的异常性，为每个测试样本计算异常分数。

Result: 在24个表格数据集上对15个SOTA基线进行对比，结果显示在混合类型和纯连续型设置下均取得一致性改进，同时消除了LLM微调和神经网络训练需求，显著降低计算成本。

Conclusion: LLM-Detector通过提示条件化的代码生成实现高效、可部署的表格异常检测，验证了LLM在无需训练的情况下能有效编码正常状态知识并执行结构化的异常评分，为实际系统提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data is challenging because abnormal samples often arise as violations of cross-feature dependencies rather than simple marginal deviations. Existing detectors rely on geometric or reconstruction signals, while prior LLM-based approaches mainly fine-tune LLMs with normal samples or generate synthetic anomalies. We propose LLM-Detector, a framework that utilizes the in-context learning capacity of LLMs for structured, prompt-conditioned scoring synthesis, enabling LLMs to derive anomaly detection logic from structured normal-state knowledge. Specifically, normal training data are converted into statistical summaries, causal dependencies, and distilled prototypes that are organized into a prompt for code generation. The resulting scoring engine evaluates statistical deviation, structural inconsistency, and density-based abnormality then computes an anomaly score for each test sample. We evaluate LLM-Detector on 24 tabular datasets, comparing against 15 SOTA baselines. Results show consistent improvements across both mixed-type and continuous-only settings. Moreover, this design eliminates the need for LLM fine-tuning or neural network training, reducing computational cost and enabling practical anomaly detection in real-world tabular systems.

</details>


### [107] [From Retrieved Context to Runtime Control: Adaptive Compression for Edge-based RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19535)
*Zlatan Feric,Amir Taherin,Yanzhi Wang,David Kaeli*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种基于遥测的自适应压缩策略用于边缘RAG，通过动态调整压缩率平衡性能与能效，实验显示中等压缩可显著降低能耗且几乎不影响质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG上下文压缩方法使用固定预算或离线选择的压缩率，忽略工作负载变化和边缘设备实时状态，导致能效优化不足或质量损失。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上，使用Llama/Qwen生成器、NQ/HotpotQA数据集和LLMLingua-2压缩器，测量不同压缩率下的延迟、GPU/SoC能耗，并分析压缩率的影响。

Result: 生成阶段占RAG延迟约90%和GPU能耗91%；中等压缩可降低GPU能耗53.2%、SoC能耗48.2%，且质量损失可忽略；过度压缩会损害质量。

Conclusion: 应设计运行时策略，根据工作负载特征和边缘遥测动态调整压缩率，以实现能效与质量的平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language-model responses by grounding generation in external passages, which comes with overhead: retrieved context lengthens the prompt, increasing prefill work, KV-cache footprint, memory traffic, latency, and energy. Context compression offers a natural remedy by pruning retrieved text before generation. However, state-of-the-art context-compression methods are typically used with a fixed compression budget, or with the rate selected offline and then applied at inference time. This static view ignores both workload variation and the live state of the edge device. On an edge SoC, compression is not free: the compressor itself runs on the same SoC and consumes latency and energy that can offset any generation savings.
  This paper proposes a vision for telemetry-informed adaptive compression in edge RAG, grounded in experimental evidence. We characterize the compression tradeoff on the NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor using Llama and Qwen generators, Natural Questions and HotpotQA datasets, and LLMLingua-2 compression. Our measurements show that generation dominates the RAG budget for larger models, reaching roughly 90% of per-query latency and 91% of GPU energy for 7B-8B generators. Exploring the impact of the compression rate reveals an adaptive operating region: mild compression can miss energy opportunities, and overly aggressive compression can hurt inference quality. Intermediate compression can reduce GPU energy by up to 53.2%, and SoC energy by up to 48.2%, with negligible quality loss. We argue for runtime policies that dynamically manage compression, guided by workload features and edge telemetry.

</details>


### [108] [When to Retrain: An Empirical Study of Retraining Policies for Streaming ML Under Concept Drift, Budget, and Latency Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19488)
*Sawan Dasari*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 概念漂移下，增量学习比重训练策略更关键；无增量更新时，周期性重训练优于反应式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产机器学习系统在概念漂移下性能下降，但缺乏重训练时机指导；重训练有成本和延迟，现有研究未系统比较策略。

Method: 统一系统模型下对比三种重训练策略（周期、误差阈值、ADWIN触发）与不重训练基线，跨3933次实验、多漂移/预算/延迟/数据集/学习模式。

Result: 增量学习时策略无显著差异；无增量时策略影响15-55个百分点，周期性训练在突变/渐变漂移下最优，反应式仅在循环漂移占优；发现延迟-预算队列交互使有效预算减半。

Conclusion: 部署模型是否增量学习是首要设计决策；无增量时选周期性重训练；反应式策略有系统性缺陷，需谨慎使用；已开源模拟器与数据。

Abstract: Production machine learning systems degrade under concept drift, yet practitioners have little principled guidance on when to retrain. Retraining is costly, retraining budgets are finite, and a retrained model does not take effect instantly: training and deployment latency leave a stale model serving predictions while the data continues to move. We present a controlled empirical study of three practical model-refresh policies (periodic retraining, error-threshold triggering, and statistical drift-triggered retraining with ADWIN) against a no-retrain baseline, evaluated under a unified system model that makes retraining budgets and training-plus-deployment latency explicit. Across 3,933 experiment runs spanning three drift regimes, three budget levels, up to five latency levels, three datasets, and two learning modes, we find that the single most consequential design decision is not the retraining policy but whether the deployed model learns incrementally. With per-sample incremental updates, and for the linear online learner with immediate labels studied here, no policy differs from the no-retrain baseline by a practically significant margin in any of 54 paired comparisons, even at extreme latency. Without incremental updates, policy choice separates outcomes by 15-55 percentage points of post-drift accuracy, and simple periodic retraining significantly outperforms both reactive policies under abrupt and gradual drift, while reactive policies retain an advantage only under recurring drift. We document systematic failure modes of reactive policies and a latency-budget queueing interaction that silently halves effective retraining budgets, and release the full simulator, dataset pipelines, and per-run artifacts for reproducibility.

</details>


### [109] [DeltaMomentum: A Key-Value based Anisotropic Momentum Update via Delta Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19491)
*Euijin Hong,Guannan Qu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DeltaMomentum，将方向感知引入动量更新规则，通过delta规则替代EMA，在多种模型和任务上显著加速收敛并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器使用EMA作为动量，对所有方向以固定速率遗忘，但训练数据通常高度各向异性，导致对频繁出现的方向记忆不足、对稀疏方向记忆过久。

Method: 利用线性层梯度可分解为输入（键）和输出侧误差（值）的结构，采用规范的delta规则更新动量缓冲区，使遗忘速率与方向出现频率自适应，无需矩阵求逆即可实现输入侧曲率校正。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu预训练中，DeltaAdamW相比AdamW少用最多46.39%的步骤达到相同验证损失；在1B规模下收益持续；在SGD、ResNet-18、ViT-Tiny等场景中也均有效，额外计算开销仅占线性层成本的22.2%-25.0%。

Conclusion: DeltaMomentum是一种即插即用的动量替代方案，能有效应对各向异性梯度，提升优化效率，且计算开销可控，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Most modern optimizers form their momentum as an exponential moving average (EMA) of past gradients, forgetting every direction at one fixed rate. However, the inputs a deep network sees during training can be highly anisotropic, with a few directions queried frequently while most are seen rarely. Recent methods address this anisotropy by wrapping extra processing around this buffer, leaving the momentum update itself unchanged. We propose DeltaMomentum, which builds direction-awareness into the momentum update rule. The main observation is that the gradient of a linear layer splits into an input that acts as a key and an output-side error that acts as a value. Exploiting the key-value structure, DeltaMomentum updates the momentum buffer by the canonical delta rule, so each direction is forgotten at a rate set by how often it appears. We prove that it is a valid momentum, that it applies the input-side curvature correction without matrix inversion, and that it clears stale directions faster than EMA under both a fixed and a drifting optimum. It is a drop-in replacement for the momentum buffer of any optimizer, its coefficient transfers across widths under $μ$P, and its extra compute stays between $22.2\%$ and $25.0\%$ of a gated-MLP block's linear cost with no persistent memory. In FineWeb-Edu pretraining, AdamW with DeltaMomentum (DeltaAdamW) reaches AdamW's validation loss in up to $46.39 \pm 4.32\%$ fewer steps at 67M and $22.12 \pm 0.80\%$ at 370M over three seeds, and the gain persists at 1B on a Chinchilla-optimal budget. A Muon baseline tuned under the same protocol sits above DeltaAdamW at both language-model scales, and the gain holds for SGD, ResNet-18, and ViT-Tiny on CIFAR-10. Training-time diagnostics confirm the predicted mechanism, better gradient tracking and healthier input directions.

</details>


### [110] [Can Agent Memory Systems Track Evolving State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19652)
*Xinyi Fan,Miri Liu,Ruozhen Yang,Siru Ouyang,Jiawei Han*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出StateMemBench基准测试，验证LLM智能体在长交互中需追踪世界状态变化而非仅回忆；StateMem方法使当前状态准确率提升1.8倍，且作为轻量包装器可普遍增强现有记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆基准侧重回忆型任务，忽略了状态追踪能力——即事实、约束和决策在长交互中不断修订，回答必须反映当前状态而非被取代的旧状态。

Method: 构建234个多会话场景的StateMemBench，采用闭池评分区分当前状态、旧状态与错误；提出StateMem，显式追踪状态取代关系和依赖关系，并可作为单次调用的轻量包装器集成到现有记忆系统。

Result: StateMem在DeepSeek-V4-Flash上将当前状态准确率从0.205提升至0.363，较同骨干最强基线提升1.8倍；在Qwen-3.5-9B上较最强记忆系统提升1.6倍（0.149→0.233）；作为包装器在六种后端上提升+32至+67点，其中+15至+32点归因于状态结构而非额外上下文。

Conclusion: 状态追踪是长周期智能体记忆的关键缺口；显式状态结构能显著提升当前状态准确性，且方法轻量、通用，但对长上下文基线仍保持竞争力，提示状态建模应作为记忆系统设计的核心组件。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents are deployed for longer and higher-stakes tasks, their memory systems continue to have crucial gaps. While existing memory benchmarks focus largely on recall-shaped tasks, we argue an effective memory system must track the evolving state of the world; as facts, constraints, and decisions are revised over a long interaction, answers must reflect the current state and not a superseded one. We define this capability as state tracking and instantiate it in StateMemBench, a benchmark of 234 multi-session scenarios spanning two conversation-length regimes. Its closed-pool grading scores whether an answer reflects the current state, the superseded state, or fails otherwise, separating state-tracking failures from other errors by construction. Our analysis shows that this task is challenging for existing memory systems, retrieval-augmented baselines, and long-context baselines. We then present StateMem, a state-first memory method that explicitly tracks supersession and relational dependencies, and show it improves current-state accuracy over the strongest same-backbone baseline by 1.8x (0.205 -> 0.363) on DeepSeek-V4-Flash and over the strongest memory system by 1.6x (0.149 -> 0.233) on Qwen-3.5-9B, while remaining competitive with the long-context baselines. Finally, we show the same state approach can be applied as a lightweight single-call wrapper over existing memory systems, lifting current-state accuracy by +32 to +67 points on StateMemBench across six memory and retrieval backends. A length- and cost-matched control attributes +15 to +32 of those points to state structure rather than added context.

</details>


### [111] [TrustRAG: Blockchain-Enhanced RAG via Committee-Based Credibility Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20097)
*Baixiang Liu,Haotian Che,Yuan Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: TrustRAG是一个基于委员会和区块链的RAG系统，通过零知识证明与安全多方计算生成可验证的信任评分，并利用跨链哈希承诺确保文档与评分的不可篡改性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖集中式数据库，缺乏对文档来源、完整性和可信度的有效验证，在医疗、金融、物流和法律等关键领域，错误或篡改的检索内容可能导致严重决策失误。

Method: 文档使用前由领域专家委员会通过零知识协议进行认证，委员会隐藏评分经安全多方计算聚合为可公开验证的信任分；文档数据与评分通过跨链哈希承诺共同维护，支持排名的独立重放与检查。

Result: 系统实现了文档与评分的防篡改、防丢失及排名可验证性，为RAG提供可信检索基础。

Conclusion: TrustRAG通过密码学与区块链机制增强了RAG系统的可信任度，特别适用于对时效性和准确性要求严格的高风险场景。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) lets Large Language Models (LLMs) pull in up-to-date, domain-specific information instead of relying only on what they were trained on. Yet most RAG systems still draw from centralized databases with limited oversight, making it difficult to verify where a document came from, whether it has been tampered with, or whether it should be trusted at all. This is a serious problem in domains where both the timeliness and accuracy of retrieved content are critical, such as healthcare, finance, logistics, and legal case law, where a wrong or manipulated document can directly lead to bad decisions.
  We present TrustRAG, a committee-based, blockchain-backed RAG system: before a document is used, it is certified by a committee of domain experts through a zero-knowledge protocol, and the committee's hidden scores are combined via secure multi-party computation into a trust score that any client can verify. These scores, along with the underlying document data, are maintained jointly across chains through hash commitments, so no document or score can be silently altered or dropped, and every ranking can be independently replayed and checked.

</details>


### [112] [In Two Minds about Lifelong Learning: Exploring Hemispheric Redundancy and Specialisation in Neural Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19514)
*Benjamin Smith,Levin Kuhlmann,Kaushik Roy,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受生物记忆巩固机制启发的持续学习架构4MAS，通过非对称双半球和睡眠期重放机制，在多个数据集上取得竞争性准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习在持续学习中面临灾难性遗忘问题，而生物系统能自然实现持续学习；同时，原始数据不可用时的重训练不切实际，亟需一种模拟生物记忆巩固的高层框架。

Method: 构建4MAS宏架构，包含非对称双半球（各自有长期和短期记忆模块），并在增量任务间引入模拟睡眠的阶段，结合经验重放、REM睡眠和双侧性概念来巩固知识。

Result: 在Split-MNIST、Split-Fashion-MNIST和Split-CIFAR-100上分别达到98.3%、84.9%和29.29%的准确率。

Conclusion: 验证了生物启发式设计（非对称结构和睡眠周期）能有效缓解灾难性遗忘，为持续学习系统提供新型宏观架构方向。

Abstract: Persistent intelligent systems require the ability to learn continually, but current machine learning approaches face significant challenges in this area compared to biological learning systems. Machine learning algorithms typically trade off retention of previously learned information and adaptation to new or changing data patterns. When continual learning capabilities are absent, algorithms must undergo retraining using the entire data set, an approach that becomes impractical when original training data are unavailable due to storage constraints, financial or computational costs, or privacy restrictions. However, biological animals can learn continually, without experiencing catastrophic forgetting. This paper attempts to build a high-level framework for how animals learn and preserve knowledge by modelling neural components and states that are known to be related to memory consolidation. We focus on three concepts: experience replay, REM sleep, and bilaterality. We propose 4MAS (4 Module Awake/Sleep), a novel macroarchitecture demonstrating how machine learning models might benefit from asymmetric hemispheres, each with their own long- and short-term memory mechanisms, and how a period of sleep between incremental learning tasks might benefit memory consolidation. Finally, we present results showing that our architecture achieves competitive results on the Split-MNIST, Split-Fashion-MNIST and Split-CIFAR-100 datasets, with 98.3%, 84.9%, and 29.29% accuracy respectively.

</details>


### [113] [Privacy-Preserving Detection of Rare Disease-Associated Cell Subsets via Secure Multi-Party Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20118)
*Ş. Selcan Magara,Esther Havemann,Debora Jutz,Ali Burak Ünal,Mete Akgün*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于安全多方计算（MPC）的框架，用于在秘密共享数据上训练和推理CellCnn模型，在不泄露原始患者数据的前提下，实现了接近明文模型的准确率，并优于先前的隐私保护基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测罕见疾病相关细胞亚群需要大规模、多样化的患者队列，但单细胞数据过于敏感，无法跨机构共享。现有隐私保护方法会移除ReLU激活和偏置项等关键组件，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 设计了一个MPC框架，使CellCnn的完整架构（包括ReLU和偏置项）能在秘密共享数据上安全训练和推理，确保参与方和计算服务器均无法访问原始数据或中间值。

Result: 在CMV和AML基准数据集上，该框架的准确率接近明文版本，且优于先前的隐私保护基线。

Conclusion: 该方法首次在保留完整CellCnn架构的前提下实现了隐私保护的单细胞分析，为跨机构协作研究罕见疾病提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The detection of rare disease-associated cell subsets from high-dimensional single-cell measurements is critical for understanding diseases such as leukaemia and viral infections. CellCnn, a convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for this task, has demonstrated the ability to identify phenotype-associated cell populations at frequencies as low as 0.01\%. Training such models reliably requires patient cohorts that are larger and more diverse than any single institution can typically assemble, and the underlying single-cell data is too sensitive to share across institutional boundaries under existing privacy regulations. We propose a secure multi-party computation (MPC) framework that enables the training and inference of CellCnn entirely on secret-shared data. This ensures that neither the participants nor the computing servers ever observe raw patient data or intermediate values.
